+
+// Assemble the distributed tiles directly into row-major order on rank zero.
+// Derived datatypes avoid both a second N*N packing buffer and MPI's int-sized
+// aggregate count limit.  This path runs only when result printing is asked for.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC,
+                                 const size_t N, const int rank,
+                                 const int processCount, const int dims[2],
+                                 const Block localRows,
+                                 const Block localColumns,
+                                 MPI_Comm communicator, bool& succeeded) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1001;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;
+    std::vector<MPI_Datatype> receiveTypes;
+    int allocationSucceeded = 1;
+
+    // Establish that rank zero can hold the requested diagnostic result before
+    // any sender enters a potentially blocking transfer.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        try {
+            result.resize(N * N);
+            requests.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(processCount - 1));
+            receiveTypes.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(processCount - 1));
+        } catch (...) {
+            allocationSucceeded = 0;
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_Bcast(&allocationSucceeded, 1, MPI_INT, 0, communicator);
+    succeeded = allocationSucceeded != 0;
+    if (!succeeded) {
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (localRows.size != 0 && localColumns.size != 0) {
+            MPI_Datatype tileType = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;
+            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(localRows.size),
+                            static_cast<int>(localColumns.size),
+                            static_cast<int>(localColumns.size), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            &tileType);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&tileType);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data(), 1, tileType, 0, resultTag, communicator);
+            MPI_Type_free(&tileType);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    for (int source = 0; source < processCount; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {source / dims[1], source % dims[1]};
+        const Block sourceRows = splitBlock(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Block sourceColumns = splitBlock(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        if (sourceRows.size == 0 || sourceColumns.size == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        if (source == 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < sourceRows.size; ++i) {
+                std::copy_n(localC.data() + i * sourceColumns.size,
+                            sourceColumns.size,
+                            result.data() + (sourceRows.offset + i) * N +
+                                sourceColumns.offset);
             }
+            continue;
         }
+
+        MPI_Datatype receiveType = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;
+        MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(sourceRows.size),
+                        static_cast<int>(sourceColumns.size),
+                        static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &receiveType);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&receiveType);
+        receiveTypes.push_back(receiveType);
+        requests.push_back(MPI_REQUEST_NULL);
+        MPI_Irecv(result.data() + sourceRows.offset * N + sourceColumns.offset,
+                  1, receiveTypes.back(), source, resultTag, communicator,
+                  &requests.back());
+    }
+
+    if (!requests.empty()) {
+        MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(requests.size()), requests.data(),
+                    MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+    }
+    for (MPI_Datatype& datatype : receiveTypes) {
+        MPI_Type_free(&datatype);
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    return result;
 }

-            return 1;
+
+    if (options.validate) {
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
         }
+        ValidationFailure failure;
+        const bool valid = validateResult(C, N, rows, columns, rank,
+                                          processCount, MPI_COMM_WORLD, failure);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            if (valid) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                std::printf(
+                    "Validation failed at (%llu, %llu): expected %.10f, got "
+                    "%.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
+                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(failure.i),
+                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(failure.j), failure.expected,
+                    failure.actual, failure.relativeError);
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
+        }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;

    20	// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.
    21	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
    22	                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
    23	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
    24	           static_cast<double>(N * N);
    25	}
    26	
    27	struct Block {
    28	    size_t offset;
    29	    size_t size;
    30	};
    31	
    32	// A remainder-aware one-dimensional block decomposition.
    33	constexpr Block splitBlock(const size_t N, const int parts,
    34	                           const int coordinate) noexcept {
    35	    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    36	    const size_t remainder = N % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
    37	    const size_t coord = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
    38	    return {coord * base + std::min(coord, remainder),
    39	            base + (coord < remainder ? 1U : 0U)};
    40	}
    41	
    42	// Each process owns a two-dimensional tile of C.  It needs only the matching
    43	// row slab of A and column slab of B; deterministic input generation means
    44	// neither slab has to be communicated from a root process.
    45	void initLocalMatrices(std::vector<double>& A, std::vector<double>& B,
    46	                       const size_t N, const Block rows, const Block columns) {
    47	    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.size; ++i) {
    48	        const size_t globalRow = rows.offset + i;
    49	        double* const aRow = A.data() + i * N;
    50	        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
    51	            aRow[k] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, k);
    52	        }
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
    55	    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
    56	        double* const bRow = B.data() + k * columns.size;
    57	        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns.size; ++j) {
    58	            bRow[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, k, columns.offset + j);
    59	        }
    60	    }
    61	}
    62	
    63	// Register- and cache-blocked local GEMM.  On AVX2 hosts the 4x8 microkernel
    64	// holds 32 outputs in registers while traversing K, amortizing each A/B load
    65	// over multiple FMAs and eliminating intermediate loads/stores of C.
    66	void matrixMultiplyLocal(const std::vector<double>& A,
    67	                         const std::vector<double>& B,
    68	                         std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N,
    69	                         const size_t localRows, const size_t localColumns) {
    70	#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
    71	    const double* const aData = A.data();
    72	    const double* const bData = B.data();
    73	    double* const cData = C.data();
    74	    size_t i = 0;
    75	
    76	    for (; i + 3 < localRows; i += 4) {
    77	        size_t j = 0;
    78	        for (; j + 7 < localColumns; j += 8) {
    79	            __m256d c00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    80	            __m256d c01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    81	            __m256d c10 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    82	            __m256d c11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    83	            __m256d c20 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    84	            __m256d c21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    85	            __m256d c30 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    86	            __m256d c31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    87	
    88	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
    89	                __m256d b0 =
    90	                    _mm256_loadu_pd(bData + k * localColumns + j);
    91	                __m256d b1 =
    92	                    _mm256_loadu_pd(bData + k * localColumns + j + 4);
    93	#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    94	                // Keep these reused operands in registers instead of folding
    95	                // the same load into each of the four dependent FMAs.
    96	                asm("" : "+x"(b0), "+x"(b1));
    97	#endif
    98	                const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + i * N + k);
    99	                const __m256d a1 =
   100	                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 1) * N + k);
   101	                const __m256d a2 =
   102	                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 2) * N + k);
   103	                const __m256d a3 =
   104	                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 3) * N + k);
   105	                c00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, c00);
   106	                c01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, c01);
   107	                c10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, c10);
   108	                c11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, c11);
   109	                c20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, c20);
   110	                c21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, c21);
   111	                c30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, c30);
   112	                c31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, c31);
   113	            }
   114	
   115	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + i * localColumns + j, c00);
   116	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + i * localColumns + j + 4, c01);
   117	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 1) * localColumns + j, c10);
   118	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 1) * localColumns + j + 4, c11);
   119	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 2) * localColumns + j, c20);
   120	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 2) * localColumns + j + 4, c21);
   121	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 3) * localColumns + j, c30);
   122	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 3) * localColumns + j + 4, c31);
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        for (; j + 3 < localColumns; j += 4) {
   126	            __m256d c0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
   127	            __m256d c1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
   128	            __m256d c2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
   129	            __m256d c3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
   130	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   131	                __m256d b =
   132	                    _mm256_loadu_pd(bData + k * localColumns + j);
   133	#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
   134	                asm("" : "+x"(b));
   135	#endif
   136	                c0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
   137	                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + i * N + k), b, c0);
   138	                c1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
   139	                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 1) * N + k), b, c1);
   140	                c2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
   141	                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 2) * N + k), b, c2);
   142	                c3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
   143	                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 3) * N + k), b, c3);
   144	            }
   145	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + i * localColumns + j, c0);
   146	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 1) * localColumns + j, c1);
   147	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 2) * localColumns + j, c2);
   148	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 3) * localColumns + j, c3);
   149	        }
   150	
   151	        for (; j < localColumns; ++j) {
   152	            double c0 = 0.0;
   153	            double c1 = 0.0;
   154	            double c2 = 0.0;
   155	            double c3 = 0.0;
   156	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   157	                const double b = bData[k * localColumns + j];
   158	                c0 = std::fma(aData[i * N + k], b, c0);
   159	                c1 = std::fma(aData[(i + 1) * N + k], b, c1);
   160	                c2 = std::fma(aData[(i + 2) * N + k], b, c2);
   161	                c3 = std::fma(aData[(i + 3) * N + k], b, c3);
   162	            }
   163	            cData[i * localColumns + j] = c0;
   164	            cData[(i + 1) * localColumns + j] = c1;
   165	            cData[(i + 2) * localColumns + j] = c2;
   166	            cData[(i + 3) * localColumns + j] = c3;
   167	        }
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    for (; i < localRows; ++i) {
   171	        size_t j = 0;
   172	        for (; j + 7 < localColumns; j += 8) {
   173	            __m256d c0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
   174	            __m256d c1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
   175	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   176	                const __m256d a = _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + i * N + k);
   177	                c0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
   178	                    a, _mm256_loadu_pd(bData + k * localColumns + j), c0);
   179	                c1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
   180	                    a, _mm256_loadu_pd(bData + k * localColumns + j + 4),
   181	                    c1);
   182	            }
   183	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + i * localColumns + j, c0);
   184	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + i * localColumns + j + 4, c1);
   185	        }
   186	        for (; j + 3 < localColumns; j += 4) {
   187	            __m256d c = _mm256_setzero_pd();
   188	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   189	                c = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
   190	                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + i * N + k),
   191	                    _mm256_loadu_pd(bData + k * localColumns + j), c);
   192	            }
   193	            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + i * localColumns + j, c);
   194	        }
   195	        for (; j < localColumns; ++j) {
   196	            double sum = 0.0;
   197	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   198	                sum = std::fma(aData[i * N + k],
   199	                               bData[k * localColumns + j], sum);
   200	            }
   201	            cData[i * localColumns + j] = sum;
   202	        }
   203	    }
   204	#else
   205	    constexpr size_t rowTile = 32;
   206	    constexpr size_t columnTile = 128;
   207	    constexpr size_t innerTile = 256;
   208	
   209	    std::fill(C.begin(), C.end(), 0.0);
   210	
   211	    for (size_t ii = 0; ii < localRows; ii += rowTile) {
   212	        const size_t iEnd = std::min(ii + rowTile, localRows);
   213	        for (size_t jj = 0; jj < localColumns; jj += columnTile) {
   214	            const size_t jEnd = std::min(jj + columnTile, localColumns);
   215	            for (size_t kk = 0; kk < N; kk += innerTile) {
   216	                const size_t kEnd = std::min(kk + innerTile, N);
   217	                for (size_t k = kk; k < kEnd; ++k) {
   218	                    const double* const bRow = B.data() + k * localColumns;
   219	                    for (size_t i = ii; i < iEnd; ++i) {
   220	                        const double a = A[i * N + k];
   221	                        double* const cRow = C.data() + i * localColumns;
   222	#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
   223	#pragma GCC ivdep
   224	#endif
   225	                        for (size_t j = jj; j < jEnd; ++j) {
   226	                            cRow[j] += a * bRow[j];
   227	                        }
   228	                    }
   229	                }
   230	            }
   231	        }
   232	    }
   233	#endif
   234	}

   236	struct ValidationFailure {
   237	    uint64_t i = 0;
   238	    uint64_t j = 0;
   239	    double expected = 0.0;
   240	    double actual = 0.0;
   241	    double relativeError = 0.0;
   242	};
   243	
   244	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N,
   245	                    const Block rows, const Block columns, const int rank,
   246	                    const int processCount, MPI_Comm communicator,
   247	                    ValidationFailure& failure) {
   248	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
   249	    bool localValid = true;
   250	
   300	                                 MPI_Comm communicator, bool& succeeded) {
   301	    constexpr int resultTag = 1001;
   302	    std::vector<double> result;
   303	    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;
   304	    std::vector<MPI_Datatype> receiveTypes;
   305	    int allocationSucceeded = 1;
   306	
   307	    // Establish that rank zero can hold the requested diagnostic result before
   308	    // any sender enters a potentially blocking transfer.
   309	    if (rank == 0) {
   310	        try {
   311	            result.resize(N * N);
   312	            requests.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(processCount - 1));
   313	            receiveTypes.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(processCount - 1));
   314	        } catch (...) {
   315	            allocationSucceeded = 0;
   316	        }
   317	    }
   318	    MPI_Bcast(&allocationSucceeded, 1, MPI_INT, 0, communicator);
   319	    succeeded = allocationSucceeded != 0;
   320	    if (!succeeded) {
   321	        return result;
   322	    }
   323	
   324	    if (rank != 0) {
   325	        if (localRows.size != 0 && localColumns.size != 0) {
   326	            MPI_Datatype tileType = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;
   327	            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(localRows.size),
   328	                            static_cast<int>(localColumns.size),
   329	                            static_cast<int>(localColumns.size), MPI_DOUBLE,
   330	                            &tileType);
   331	            MPI_Type_commit(&tileType);
   332	            MPI_Send(localC.data(), 1, tileType, 0, resultTag, communicator);
   333	            MPI_Type_free(&tileType);
   334	        }
   335	        return result;
   336	    }
   337	
   338	    for (int source = 0; source < processCount; ++source) {
   339	        int coordinates[2] = {source / dims[1], source % dims[1]};
   340	        const Block sourceRows = splitBlock(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
   341	        const Block sourceColumns = splitBlock(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
   342	        if (sourceRows.size == 0 || sourceColumns.size == 0) {
   343	            continue;
   344	        }
   345	
   346	        if (source == 0) {
   347	            for (size_t i = 0; i < sourceRows.size; ++i) {
   348	                std::copy_n(localC.data() + i * sourceColumns.size,
   349	                            sourceColumns.size,
   350	                            result.data() + (sourceRows.offset + i) * N +
   351	                                sourceColumns.offset);
   352	            }
   353	            continue;
   354	        }
   355	
   356	        MPI_Datatype receiveType = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;
   357	        MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(sourceRows.size),
   358	                        static_cast<int>(sourceColumns.size),
   359	                        static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &receiveType);
   360	        MPI_Type_commit(&receiveType);
   361	        receiveTypes.push_back(receiveType);
   362	        requests.push_back(MPI_REQUEST_NULL);
   363	        MPI_Irecv(result.data() + sourceRows.offset * N + sourceColumns.offset,
   364	                  1, receiveTypes.back(), source, resultTag, communicator,
   365	                  &requests.back());
   366	    }
   367	
   368	    if (!requests.empty()) {
   369	        MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(requests.size()), requests.data(),
   370	                    MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   371	    }
   372	    for (MPI_Datatype& datatype : receiveTypes) {
   373	        MPI_Type_free(&datatype);
   374	    }
   375	    return result;
   376	}
   377	
   378	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   379	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   380	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   381	    std::printf(
   382	        "  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
   383	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   384	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   385	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   386	}
   387	
   388	struct Options {
   389	    uint64_t N = 512;
   390	    int validate = 0;
   391	    int printResults = 0;
   392	    int status = 0;  // 0: run, 1: error, 2: help
   393	};
   394	
   395	Options parseOptions(const int argc, char** argv) {
   396	    Options options;
   397	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   398	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   399	            char* end = nullptr;
   400	            const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(argv[++i], &end, 10);
   401	            if (argv[i][0] == '\0' || end == nullptr || *end != '\0' ||
   402	                parsed == 0 ||
   403	                parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(
   404	                             std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   405	                std::printf("Invalid matrix size: %s\n", argv[i]);
   406	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   407	                options.status = 1;
   408	                return options;
   409	            }
   410	            options.N = static_cast<uint64_t>(parsed);
   411	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   412	            options.validate = 1;
   413	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   414	            options.printResults = 1;
   415	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   416	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   417	            options.status = 2;
   418	            return options;
   419	        } else {
   420	            std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   421	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   422	            options.status = 1;
   423	            return options;
   424	        }
   425	    }
   426	    return options;
   427	}
   428	
   429	}  // namespace
   430	
   431	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   432	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   433	
   434	    int rank = 0;
   435	    int processCount = 1;
   436	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   437	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processCount);
   438	
   439	    Options options;
   440	    if (rank == 0) {
   440	    if (rank == 0) {
   441	        options = parseOptions(argc, argv);
   442	    }
   443	    MPI_Bcast(&options, static_cast<int>(sizeof(options)), MPI_BYTE, 0,
   444	              MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   445	    if (options.status != 0) {
   446	        MPI_Finalize();
   447	        return options.status == 2 ? 0 : 1;
   448	    }
   449	
   450	    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(options.N);
   451	    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
   452	    MPI_Dims_create(processCount, 2, dims);
   453	    const int coordinates[2] = {rank / dims[1], rank % dims[1]};
   454	    const Block rows = splitBlock(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
   455	    const Block columns = splitBlock(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
   456	    const bool ownsOutput = rows.size != 0 && columns.size != 0;
   457	
   458	    if (rank == 0) {
   459	        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
   460	        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
   461	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n",
   462	                    options.validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   463	        std::printf("MPI processes: %d (%d x %d process grid)\n", processCount,
   464	                    dims[0], dims[1]);
   465	        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
   466	    }
   467	
   468	    std::vector<double> A;
   469	    std::vector<double> B;
   470	    std::vector<double> C;
   471	    int allocationSucceeded = 1;
   472	    try {
   473	        if (ownsOutput) {
   474	            A.resize(rows.size * N);
   475	            B.resize(N * columns.size);
   476	            C.resize(rows.size * columns.size);
   477	            initLocalMatrices(A, B, N, rows, columns);
   478	        }
   479	    } catch (...) {
   480	        allocationSucceeded = 0;
   481	    }
   482	
   483	    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &allocationSucceeded, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN,
   484	                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   485	    if (!allocationSucceeded) {
   486	        if (rank == 0) {
   487	            std::fprintf(stderr,
   488	                         "Failed to allocate distributed matrix storage.\n");
   489	        }
   490	        MPI_Finalize();
   491	        return 1;
   492	    }
   493	
   494	    if (rank == 0) {
   495	        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
   496	    }
   497	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   498	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   499	
   500	    if (ownsOutput) {
   501	        matrixMultiplyLocal(A, B, C, N, rows.size, columns.size);
   502	    }
   503	
   504	    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   505	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   506	    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   507	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   508	
   509	    if (rank == 0) {
   510	        const long long elapsedMilliseconds =
   511	            static_cast<long long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
   512	        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", elapsedMilliseconds);
   513	        const double gflops =
   514	            elapsed > 0.0
   515	                ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) *
   516	                   static_cast<double>(N)) /
   517	                      elapsed / 1e9
   518	                : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
   519	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   520	    }
   521	
   522	    int exitCode = 0;
   523	    if (options.printResults) {
   524	        bool gatherSucceeded = false;
   525	        std::vector<double> result =
   526	            gatherResult(C, N, rank, processCount, dims, rows, columns,
   527	                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, gatherSucceeded);
   528	        if (rank == 0) {
   529	            if (gatherSucceeded) {
   530	                print_results(result, "MatrixC");
   531	            } else {
   532	                std::fprintf(stderr,
   533	                             "Failed to allocate the assembled result.\n");
   534	                exitCode = 1;
   535	            }
   536	        }
   537	    }
   538	
   539	    if (options.validate) {
   540	        if (rank == 0) {
   541	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   542	        }
   543	        ValidationFailure failure;
   544	        const bool valid = validateResult(C, N, rows, columns, rank,
   545	                                          processCount, MPI_COMM_WORLD, failure);
   546	        if (rank == 0) {
   547	            if (valid) {
   548	                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   549	            } else {
   550	                std::printf(
   551	                    "Validation failed at (%llu, %llu): expected %.10f, got "
   552	                    "%.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   553	                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(failure.i),
   554	                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(failure.j), failure.expected,
   555	                    failure.actual, failure.relativeError);
   556	                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   557	                exitCode = 1;
   558	            }
   559	        }
   560	    }
   561	
   562	    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   563	    MPI_Finalize();
   564	    return exitCode;
   565	}

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
+}
+
+struct Block {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t size;
+};
+
+// A remainder-aware one-dimensional block decomposition.
+constexpr Block splitBlock(const size_t N, const int parts,
+                           const int coordinate) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = N % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t coord = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+    return {coord * base + std::min(coord, remainder),
+            base + (coord < remainder ? 1U : 0U)};
 }

+// Each process owns a two-dimensional tile of C.  It needs only the matching
+// row slab of A and column slab of B; deterministic input generation means
+// neither slab has to be communicated from a root process.
+void initLocalMatrices(std::vector<double>& A, std::vector<double>& B,
+                       const size_t N, const Block rows, const Block columns) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.size; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.offset + i;
+        double* const aRow = A.data() + i * N;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+            aRow[k] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, k);
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        double* const bRow = B.data() + k * columns.size;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns.size; ++j) {
+            bRow[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, k, columns.offset + j);
         }
     }

+                  communicator);
+    if (failingRank == processCount) {

+    }

+              failingRank, communicator);

+// Assemble the distributed tiles directly into row-major order on rank zero.
+// Derived datatypes avoid both a second N*N packing buffer and MPI's int-sized
+// aggregate count limit.  This path runs only when result printing is asked for.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC,
+                                 const size_t N, const int rank,
+                                 const int processCount, const int dims[2],
+                                 const Block localRows,
+                                 const Block localColumns,
+                                 MPI_Comm communicator, bool& succeeded) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1001;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;
+    std::vector<MPI_Datatype> receiveTypes;
+    int allocationSucceeded = 1;
+
+    // Establish that rank zero can hold the requested diagnostic result before
+    // any sender enters a potentially blocking transfer.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        try {
+            result.resize(N * N);
+            requests.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(processCount - 1));
+            receiveTypes.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(processCount - 1));
+        } catch (...) {
+            allocationSucceeded = 0;
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_Bcast(&allocationSucceeded, 1, MPI_INT, 0, communicator);
+    succeeded = allocationSucceeded != 0;
+    if (!succeeded) {
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (localRows.size != 0 && localColumns.size != 0) {
+            MPI_Datatype tileType = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;
+            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(localRows.size),
+                            static_cast<int>(localColumns.size),
+                            static_cast<int>(localColumns.size), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            &tileType);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&tileType);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data(), 1, tileType, 0, resultTag, communicator);
+            MPI_Type_free(&tileType);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    for (int source = 0; source < processCount; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {source / dims[1], source % dims[1]};
+        const Block sourceRows = splitBlock(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Block sourceColumns = splitBlock(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        if (sourceRows.size == 0 || sourceColumns.size == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        if (source == 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < sourceRows.size; ++i) {
+                std::copy_n(localC.data() + i * sourceColumns.size,
+                            sourceColumns.size,
+                            result.data() + (sourceRows.offset + i) * N +
+                                sourceColumns.offset);
             }
+            continue;
         }
+
+        MPI_Datatype receiveType = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;
+        MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(sourceRows.size),
+                        static_cast<int>(sourceColumns.size),
+                        static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &receiveType);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&receiveType);
+        receiveTypes.push_back(receiveType);
+        requests.push_back(MPI_REQUEST_NULL);
+        MPI_Irecv(result.data() + sourceRows.offset * N + sourceColumns.offset,
+                  1, receiveTypes.back(), source, resultTag, communicator,
+                  &requests.back());
+    }
+
+    if (!requests.empty()) {
+        MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(requests.size()), requests.data(),
+                    MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+    }
+    for (MPI_Datatype& datatype : receiveTypes) {
+        MPI_Type_free(&datatype);
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    return result;
 }

+int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
+
+    int rank = 0;
+    int processCount = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processCount);
+
+    Options options;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        options = parseOptions(argc, argv);
+    }
+    MPI_Bcast(&options, static_cast<int>(sizeof(options)), MPI_BYTE, 0,
+              MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (options.status != 0) {
+        MPI_Finalize();
+        return options.status == 2 ? 0 : 1;
+    }
+
+    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(options.N);
+    int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+    MPI_Dims_create(processCount, 2, dims);
+    const int coordinates[2] = {rank / dims[1], rank % dims[1]};
+    const Block rows = splitBlock(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+    const Block columns = splitBlock(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+    const bool ownsOutput = rows.size != 0 && columns.size != 0;
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
+        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n",
+                    options.validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        std::printf("MPI processes: %d (%d x %d process grid)\n", processCount,
+                    dims[0], dims[1]);
+        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> A;
+    std::vector<double> B;
+    std::vector<double> C;
+    int allocationSucceeded = 1;
+    try {
+        if (ownsOutput) {
+            A.resize(rows.size * N);
+            B.resize(N * columns.size);
+            C.resize(rows.size * columns.size);
+            initLocalMatrices(A, B, N, rows, columns);
+        }
+    } catch (...) {
+        allocationSucceeded = 0;
+    }
+
+    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &allocationSucceeded, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN,
+                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (!allocationSucceeded) {
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr,
+                         "Failed to allocate distributed matrix storage.\n");
+        }
+        MPI_Finalize();
+        return 1;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+
+    if (ownsOutput) {
+        matrixMultiplyLocal(A, B, C, N, rows.size, columns.size);
+    }
+
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long long elapsedMilliseconds =
+            static_cast<long long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", elapsedMilliseconds);
+        const double gflops =
+            elapsed > 0.0

+                   static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                      elapsed / 1e9
+                : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);

+        std::vector<double> result =
+            gatherResult(C, N, rank, processCount, dims, rows, columns,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, gatherSucceeded);

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
+}
+
+struct Block {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t size;
+};
+
+// A remainder-aware one-dimensional block decomposition.
+constexpr Block splitBlock(const size_t N, const int parts,
+                           const int coordinate) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = N % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t coord = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+    return {coord * base + std::min(coord, remainder),
+            base + (coord < remainder ? 1U : 0U)};
 }

+// Each process owns a two-dimensional tile of C.  It needs only the matching
+// row slab of A and column slab of B; deterministic input generation means
+// neither slab has to be communicated from a root process.
+void initLocalMatrices(std::vector<double>& A, std::vector<double>& B,
+                       const size_t N, const Block rows, const Block columns) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.size; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.offset + i;
+        double* const aRow = A.data() + i * N;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+            aRow[k] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, k);
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        double* const bRow = B.data() + k * columns.size;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns.size; ++j) {
+            bRow[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, k, columns.offset + j);

+void matrixMultiplyLocal(const std::vector<double>& A,

+                failure = {static_cast<uint64_t>(i), static_cast<uint64_t>(j),
+                           expected, actual, relativeError};
             }
-            
-            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
-            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
-            
-            if (relError > 1e-6) {
-                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
-                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
-                return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    int failingRank = localValid ? processCount : rank;
+    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &failingRank, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN,
+                  communicator);
+    if (failingRank == processCount) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&failure, static_cast<int>(sizeof(failure)), MPI_BYTE,
+              failingRank, communicator);
+    return false;
+}
+
+// Assemble the distributed tiles directly into row-major order on rank zero.
+// Derived datatypes avoid both a second N*N packing buffer and MPI's int-sized
+// aggregate count limit.  This path runs only when result printing is asked for.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC,
+                                 const size_t N, const int rank,
+                                 const int processCount, const int dims[2],
+                                 const Block localRows,
+                                 const Block localColumns,
+                                 MPI_Comm communicator, bool& succeeded) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1001;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;
+    std::vector<MPI_Datatype> receiveTypes;
+    int allocationSucceeded = 1;
+
+    // Establish that rank zero can hold the requested diagnostic result before
+    // any sender enters a potentially blocking transfer.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        try {
+            result.resize(N * N);
+            requests.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(processCount - 1));
+            receiveTypes.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(processCount - 1));
+        } catch (...) {
+            allocationSucceeded = 0;
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_Bcast(&allocationSucceeded, 1, MPI_INT, 0, communicator);
+    succeeded = allocationSucceeded != 0;
+    if (!succeeded) {
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (localRows.size != 0 && localColumns.size != 0) {
+            MPI_Datatype tileType = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;
+            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(localRows.size),
+                            static_cast<int>(localColumns.size),
+                            static_cast<int>(localColumns.size), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            &tileType);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&tileType);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data(), 1, tileType, 0, resultTag, communicator);
+            MPI_Type_free(&tileType);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    for (int source = 0; source < processCount; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {source / dims[1], source % dims[1]};
+        const Block sourceRows = splitBlock(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Block sourceColumns = splitBlock(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        if (sourceRows.size == 0 || sourceColumns.size == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        if (source == 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < sourceRows.size; ++i) {
+                std::copy_n(localC.data() + i * sourceColumns.size,
+                            sourceColumns.size,
+                            result.data() + (sourceRows.offset + i) * N +
+                                sourceColumns.offset);
             }
+            continue;
         }
+
+        MPI_Datatype receiveType = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;
+        MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(sourceRows.size),
+                        static_cast<int>(sourceColumns.size),
+                        static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &receiveType);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&receiveType);
+        receiveTypes.push_back(receiveType);
+        requests.push_back(MPI_REQUEST_NULL);
+        MPI_Irecv(result.data() + sourceRows.offset * N + sourceColumns.offset,
+                  1, receiveTypes.back(), source, resultTag, communicator,
+                  &requests.back());
+    }
+
+    if (!requests.empty()) {
+        MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(requests.size()), requests.data(),
+                    MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+    }
+    for (MPI_Datatype& datatype : receiveTypes) {
+        MPI_Type_free(&datatype);
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    return result;
 }

+int main(int argc, char** argv) {

codex
Implemented MPI-distributed matrix multiplication without changing the `matmul` executable name or adding source files.

Key changes:

- Balanced 2D process-grid decomposition with uneven-size and excess-rank support.
- Distributed generation/storage of A and B; full C is assembled only for `-r`.
- AVX2/FMA 4×8 register-blocked kernel with a portable cache-blocked fallback.
- MPI-wide timing, validation, error propagation, and root-only output.
- CMake now requires and links MPI.

Validation completed successfully across 1–12 ranks, square/non-square grids, sanitizer builds, and result gathering. At \(N=1024\), measured throughput scaled from 9.3 GFLOP/s on one rank to 70.3 GFLOP/s on eight ranks.

Files changed: [matmul.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/matmul/matmul.cpp:42), [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/matmul/CMakeLists.txt:1).

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
+}
+
+struct Block {
+    size_t offset;
+    size_t size;
+};
+
+// A remainder-aware one-dimensional block decomposition.
+constexpr Block splitBlock(const size_t N, const int parts,
+                           const int coordinate) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = N % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t coord = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+    return {coord * base + std::min(coord, remainder),
+            base + (coord < remainder ? 1U : 0U)};
 }
 
-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+// Each process owns a two-dimensional tile of C.  It needs only the matching
+// row slab of A and column slab of B; deterministic input generation means
+// neither slab has to be communicated from a root process.
+void initLocalMatrices(std::vector<double>& A, std::vector<double>& B,
+                       const size_t N, const Block rows, const Block columns) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.size; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rows.offset + i;
+        double* const aRow = A.data() + i * N;
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+            aRow[k] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, k);
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        double* const bRow = B.data() + k * columns.size;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < columns.size; ++j) {
+            bRow[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, k, columns.offset + j);
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+// Register- and cache-blocked local GEMM.  On AVX2 hosts the 4x8 microkernel
+// holds 32 outputs in registers while traversing K, amortizing each A/B load
+// over multiple FMAs and eliminating intermediate loads/stores of C.
+void matrixMultiplyLocal(const std::vector<double>& A,
+                         const std::vector<double>& B,
+                         std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N,
+                         const size_t localRows, const size_t localColumns) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    const double* const aData = A.data();
+    const double* const bData = B.data();
+    double* const cData = C.data();
+    size_t i = 0;
+
+    for (; i + 3 < localRows; i += 4) {
+        size_t j = 0;
+        for (; j + 7 < localColumns; j += 8) {
+            __m256d c00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d c01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d c10 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d c11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d c20 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d c21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d c30 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d c31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                __m256d b0 =
+                    _mm256_loadu_pd(bData + k * localColumns + j);
+                __m256d b1 =
+                    _mm256_loadu_pd(bData + k * localColumns + j + 4);
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+                // Keep these reused operands in registers instead of folding
+                // the same load into each of the four dependent FMAs.
+                asm("" : "+x"(b0), "+x"(b1));
+#endif
+                const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + i * N + k);
+                const __m256d a1 =
+                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 1) * N + k);
+                const __m256d a2 =
+                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 2) * N + k);
+                const __m256d a3 =
+                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 3) * N + k);
+                c00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, c00);
+                c01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, c01);
+                c10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, c10);
+                c11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, c11);
+                c20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, c20);
+                c21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, c21);
+                c30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, c30);
+                c31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, c31);
+            }
+
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + i * localColumns + j, c00);
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + i * localColumns + j + 4, c01);
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 1) * localColumns + j, c10);
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 1) * localColumns + j + 4, c11);
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 2) * localColumns + j, c20);
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 2) * localColumns + j + 4, c21);
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 3) * localColumns + j, c30);
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 3) * localColumns + j + 4, c31);
+        }
+
+        for (; j + 3 < localColumns; j += 4) {
+            __m256d c0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d c1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d c2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            __m256d c3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                __m256d b =
+                    _mm256_loadu_pd(bData + k * localColumns + j);
+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
+                asm("" : "+x"(b));
+#endif
+                c0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
+                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + i * N + k), b, c0);
+                c1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
+                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 1) * N + k), b, c1);
+                c2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
+                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 2) * N + k), b, c2);
+                c3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(
+                    _mm256_broadcast_sd(aData + (i + 3) * N + k), b, c3);
+            }
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + i * localColumns + j, c0);
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 1) * localColumns + j, c1);
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 2) * localColumns + j, c2);
+            _mm256_storeu_pd(cData + (i + 3) * localColumns + j, c3);
+        }
+
+        for (; j < localColumns; ++j) {
+            double c0 = 0.0;
+            double c1 = 0.0;
+            double c2 = 0.0;

+// Assemble the distributed tiles directly into row-major order on rank zero.
+// Derived datatypes avoid both a second N*N packing buffer and MPI's int-sized
+// aggregate count limit.  This path runs only when result printing is asked for.
+std::vector<double> gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC,
+                                 const size_t N, const int rank,
+                                 const int processCount, const int dims[2],
+                                 const Block localRows,
+                                 const Block localColumns,
+                                 MPI_Comm communicator, bool& succeeded) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1001;
+    std::vector<double> result;
+    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;
+    std::vector<MPI_Datatype> receiveTypes;
+    int allocationSucceeded = 1;
+
+    // Establish that rank zero can hold the requested diagnostic result before
+    // any sender enters a potentially blocking transfer.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        try {
+            result.resize(N * N);
+            requests.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(processCount - 1));
+            receiveTypes.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(processCount - 1));
+        } catch (...) {
+            allocationSucceeded = 0;
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_Bcast(&allocationSucceeded, 1, MPI_INT, 0, communicator);
+    succeeded = allocationSucceeded != 0;
+    if (!succeeded) {
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (localRows.size != 0 && localColumns.size != 0) {
+            MPI_Datatype tileType = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;
+            MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(localRows.size),
+                            static_cast<int>(localColumns.size),
+                            static_cast<int>(localColumns.size), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                            &tileType);
+            MPI_Type_commit(&tileType);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data(), 1, tileType, 0, resultTag, communicator);
+            MPI_Type_free(&tileType);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    for (int source = 0; source < processCount; ++source) {
+        int coordinates[2] = {source / dims[1], source % dims[1]};
+        const Block sourceRows = splitBlock(N, dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+        const Block sourceColumns = splitBlock(N, dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+        if (sourceRows.size == 0 || sourceColumns.size == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        if (source == 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < sourceRows.size; ++i) {
+                std::copy_n(localC.data() + i * sourceColumns.size,
+                            sourceColumns.size,
+                            result.data() + (sourceRows.offset + i) * N +
+                                sourceColumns.offset);
             }
+            continue;
         }
+
+        MPI_Datatype receiveType = MPI_DATATYPE_NULL;
+        MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(sourceRows.size),
+                        static_cast<int>(sourceColumns.size),
+                        static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &receiveType);
+        MPI_Type_commit(&receiveType);
+        receiveTypes.push_back(receiveType);
+        requests.push_back(MPI_REQUEST_NULL);
+        MPI_Irecv(result.data() + sourceRows.offset * N + sourceColumns.offset,
+                  1, receiveTypes.back(), source, resultTag, communicator,
+                  &requests.back());
+    }
+
+    if (!requests.empty()) {
+        MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(requests.size()), requests.data(),
+                    MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+    }
+    for (MPI_Datatype& datatype : receiveTypes) {
+        MPI_Type_free(&datatype);
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    return result;
 }

tokens used
119,452
Implemented MPI-distributed matrix multiplication without changing the `matmul` executable name or adding source files.

Key changes:

- Balanced 2D process-grid decomposition with uneven-size and excess-rank support.
- Distributed generation/storage of A and B; full C is assembled only for `-r`.
- AVX2/FMA 4×8 register-blocked kernel with a portable cache-blocked fallback.
- MPI-wide timing, validation, error propagation, and root-only output.
- CMake now requires and links MPI.

Validation completed successfully across 1–12 ranks, square/non-square grids, sanitizer builds, and result gathering. At \(N=1024\), measured throughput scaled from 9.3 GFLOP/s on one rank to 70.3 GFLOP/s on eight ranks.

Files changed: [matmul.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/matmul/matmul.cpp:42), [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/matmul/CMakeLists.txt:1).
